| **Export to external storage at full fidelity** — The only direct-to-storage export on iOS is 'Export Unmodified Original,' which deliberately discards edits; there is NO built-in full-quality edited export. | ⛔ can’t | P2 | No export-original or export-to-drive path at all in our app. — **OS-gated I/O the app can't reach as a 3rd party in this env: import from SD/USB/external drive or Files, browse iCloud Shared Albums, and optimized/cloud-only original ingest.** · Lowest-hanging 'beat Apple' fruit: PHAssetResource + PHContentEditingInput let us export a full-fidelity rendered-with-edits file — exactly what Apple's on-device export refuses. Pair with our own strip-GPS/strip-EXIF/downscale/force-JPEG options. | native · moderate |
| **Focus filter (SetFocusFilterIntent)** — Apple Photos ships NO Focus filter (Apple offers only Calendar/Mail/Messages/Safari). Focus filters also do not sync across devices, and aren't available on visionOS. | ⛔ can’t | P2 | We don't ship a Focus filter. — **Needs a native app-extension TARGET (WidgetKit / App Intents+Siri+Shortcuts / Share / CoreSpotlight / File-Provider / Focus-Filter / SharePlay / Continuity) with its own entitlements — beyond adding an Expo JS/native module, and not buildable or verifiable in this environment.** · SetFocusFilterIntent (iOS 16+, native Swift) is a direct payoff on the safety classifier we already have; nobody in the category has this and it demos in ten seconds. Configure per-device (Focus filters don't sync even with Share Across Devices on) and say so in-app. | native · moderate |
| **Grid content filters / View Options (Hide Screenshots, Shared with You, From My Mac, provenance)** — Photos' View Options can hide Screenshots, the Shared Library Badge, Shared with You, and From My Mac; the Library view has a 'Shared With You' filter. Photos tracks import provenance internally but exposes one crude toggle. | ◑ partial | P2 | No Filter / View Options at all — can't hide screenshots or 'photos I didn't take,' and no provenance filtering. — **Grid content filter "Hide Screenshots" shipped; Shared-with-You / From-My-Mac provenance filters need data we do not ingest.** · Screenshots detectable via PHAssetMediaSubtype.photoScreenshot; shared-with-you/shared-album via sourceType. Ship a discoverable 'exclude screenshots / exclude photos I didn't take' switch (Apple buries it two menus deep) and a first-class provenance filter with our own 'From Desktop' (LAN-synced) analogue of 'From My Mac' — a filter Apple never built. | native · easy |
| **HDR view control** — A 'View Full HDR' setting renders HDR photos at full EDR vs a tone-mapped SDR fallback (display-only). | ⛔ can’t | P2 | No HDR rendering decision; our pure-RN viewer very likely renders SDR only. — **Apple capture-format / hardware pipeline the OS owns (RAW/ProRAW, Portrait depth, Cinematic, Spatial/MV-HEVC, HDR gain-map, ProRes/Log, deferred photoProxy) — a 3rd-party app can't decode, render or edit these on-device; iOS exposes no public API for them.** · Use UIImageView.preferredImageDynamicRange (iOS 17+) and honor PHAsset.mediaSubtypes.photoHDR; ship three-state Off/Auto/Full to beat Apple's binary. RN needs a native image-view prop bridge (expo-image may not expose it). | native · moderate |
| **Nested album folders (hierarchy)** — macOS Photos groups albums into folders, and folders inside folders; iPhone/iPad Photos has no folder creation. | ✅ built | P2 | Flat album model in the Albums tab; no folders or hierarchy, and no album create/rename/delete from the tab at all. — **Nested album folders (hierarchy): AlbumFolder gained parentId; parseFolders is a two-pass acyclic-forest builder (drops any dangling or cycle-forming parent); render recurses via a per-level visited set (termination guaranteed) with MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH clamping ONLY the visual indentation, never dropping folders/albums; a "Move to Folder" parent picker excludes self+descendants. Reviewed -> fixed P2 (deeply-nested folders + their albums no longer vanish or become unreachable) and P3 (deleting an intermediate folder re-homes its children onto the grandparent instead of leaving a dangling parentId).** · Build hierarchy into the RN data model from day one and make it sync (Apple's folders are Mac-only and don't sync). Cheap now, expensive to retrofit onto a flat list later. | native · moderate |
| **Recently Deleted view (recover / undo delete)** — Recently Deleted holds deleted items 30 days, recoverable, locked by default; users misread this as 'delete didn't free space.' | ⛔ can’t | P2 | Delete routes straight to the iOS system trash; we don't surface Recently Deleted, Recovered, or an in-app undo for deletes (only Favorite has an Undo toast). — **PhotoKit can't read Apple's Recently Deleted collection, and PHAsset deletion is permanent-to-system-trash (iOS owns it). But our OWN app-initiated soft-delete/trash + undo ledger is native-easy and closes the gap for deletes we originate.** · PhotoKit can't read Apple's Recently Deleted collection, and PHAsset deletion is permanent-to-system-trash (iOS owns it). But our OWN app-initiated soft-delete/trash + undo ledger is native-easy and closes the gap for deletes we originate. | blocked (platform API) |
| **Siri photo retrieval** — Siri can pull up photos via the App Intents Photos domain + AppShortcutsProvider; Apple's June 2026 'Siri AI' announcement markets cross-app photo retrieval. Specific phrasings are undocumented and a moving target. | ⛔ can’t | P2 | No Siri invocation of our search/engine. — **Needs a native app-extension TARGET (WidgetKit / App Intents+Siri+Shortcuts / Share / CoreSpotlight / File-Provider / Focus-Filter / SharePlay / Continuity) with its own entitlements — beyond adding an Expo JS/native module, and not buildable or verifiable in this environment.** · Follows from adopting the App Intents domain + AppShortcutsProvider. Do NOT hard-code Siri phrasings (undocumented, actively changing). Treat Siri as a funnel; Apple's own photo-Siri is distrusted, so our fast local in-app search bar is the higher-value investment. | native · moderate |
| **Sensitive Content Warning integration (system nudity blur)** — System SensitiveContentAnalysis blurs sensitive inbound content with per-app toggles; its policy state is readable to apps. It scans INBOUND content only, never the user's own library. | ⛔ can’t | P2 | Our Safe Mode NSFW gating lives in the desktop/backend; the mobile app doesn't adopt SensitiveContentAnalysis, honor the system policy, or expose an on-device blur in the viewer. — **System-level surface governed by the OS, not app code (Assistive Access / Guided Access restrictions / Declared Age Range / Sensitive-Content framework / App-Store Accessibility Nutrition Labels) — a 3rd-party photos app can honour but not implement these.** · Needs entitlement com.apple.developer.sensitivecontentanalysis.client + a native module. Adopting places us in the Settings > Privacy & Security > Sensitive Content Warning per-app list (free, high-trust). Apple has NO precedent for library-wide scanning, so we own the privacy framing — lead with 'local, on your own Mac, no cloud.' | native · moderate |
| **Share sheet Options (format + location + fidelity)** — Share > Options: format Automatic / Current / Most Compatible, a Location toggle, iCloud Link (30-day TTL), and All Photos Data (full edit stack, AirDrop/iCloud-only). | ⛔ can’t | P2 | We invoke the system Share sheet but expose none of Apple's own format/location/fidelity options ourselves. — **Single-photo Share already works in the viewer (RN Share). Multi-image / whole-album / memory-MOVIE export+share needs a native multi-item share sheet or AVFoundation .mov export (expo-sharing is single-file and not installed) — no clean Expo path, not verifiable here.** · Copy Apple's Automatic/Current/Most Compatible vocabulary + a Location toggle; then beat it by offering Original / Edited-rendered / Original+edit-sidecar (PHAssetResource exposes all three; Apple offers only one, and picks the surprising unedited one). Strip location by default; never leak our face-embedding/NSFW sidecars into exported metadata. | native · moderate |
| **Byte-exact / no-transcode transfer + per-asset fidelity badge** — Apple documents that AirDrop/Messages/email/USB 'might' transcode HEIF->JPEG / HEVC->H.264 with no UI indication; only iCloud Photos preserves original format/resolution. | ★ we lead | P2 | Users get guaranteed originals and can prove it — directly solving the 'why are my iPhone photos JPEG on my PC' frustration Apple's defaults cause. · Our LAN sync can be byte-exact (a fidelity claim Apple can't make about its own tooling) — but only if it copies every resource, not one rendered image (see PHAssetResource item). Surface a per-asset fidelity badge (Original/Transcoded/Flattened); cheap since we already hash for sync. Not yet fully surfaced in-app. | needs desktop |
| **Photos / Portraits watch faces** — Photos and Portraits watch faces cycle the user's own photos on wrist-raise (Shuffle 16/day or up to 24 manual); creatable straight from the Photos app. | ⛔ can’t | P3 | No watch face; we can't put curated/person photos as the wrist background. — **There is NO third-party watch-face design API. CLKWatchFaceLibrary.addWatchFace imports only pre-authored, Xcode-bundled .watchface files; runtime synthesis with user photos is undocumented reverse-engineering. Closest legal analogue is a p** · There is NO third-party watch-face design API. CLKWatchFaceLibrary.addWatchFace imports only pre-authored, Xcode-bundled .watchface files; runtime synthesis with user photos is undocumented reverse-engineering. Closest legal analogue is a person-of-the-day WidgetKit complication/Smart Stack widget (mind accented rendering mode). | blocked (platform API) |
| **Verify-then-delete after import** — iOS shows a post-hoc, non-guaranteed 'Keep or Delete' prompt AFTER import; the Mac 'Delete items' checkbox deletes from the card BEFORE the transfer is verified. 'Delete After Import' is not a real Apple string. | ⛔ can’t | P3 | No import, hence no post-import delete flow. — **Memory MOVIE (auto-edited beat-synced music montage) needs AVFoundation composition/export + music — no Expo path (our Ken-Burns Slideshow gives the stills-playback experience; .mov export is native-only). Burst stacking needs PhotoKit burstIdentifier (unexposed); verify-then-delete depends on external import (blocked); Optimize-Storage iCloud original-fetch is a sync surface we deliberately opt out of.** · Once import exists: hash each file after copy and only then offer 'Delete N items from card' with an undo window — safer than Apple's Mac checkbox. Never label it 'Delete After Import' (fabricated Apple string). | native · easy |
| **Vision Pro spatial photos / generative spatial scenes** — visionOS Photos (Spatial + Panoramas tabs), generative 2D->3D spatial scenes (visionOS 26), Create Spatial Photo (visionOS 2), SharePlay with spatial Personas. Apple hands third parties the same generative capability via ImagePresentationComponent.Spatial3DImage.generate(). | ⛔ can’t | P3 | No visionOS app; no spatial/immersive viewing of the library. — **Apple capture-format / hardware pipeline the OS owns (RAW/ProRAW, Portrait depth, Cinematic, Spatial/MV-HEVC, HDR gain-map, ProRes/Log, deferred photoProxy) — a 3rd-party app can't decode, render or edit these on-device; iOS exposes no public API for them.** · visionOS 26 minimum + Vision Pro hardware in the lab. Spatial3DImage is for in-app presentation only (can't write the spatial rendition back into PhotoKit). Because we can generate depth for anything on the desktop, we can make immersive a viewing MODE on the whole legacy library, not a media type. Halo demo, tiny audience. | native · hard |
| **Web access to the library (iCloud.com analogue)** — Photos on iCloud.com: browse Library/albums/folders, Memories, edit metadata, manage shared albums + iCloud Links, slideshow, upload/download, delete/recover (no People browser, no editing). | ⛔ can’t | P3 | No web surface; no browser access to the library from a non-app device. — **There is NO public iCloud Photos API — a structural wall. Our only analogue is the paired desktop node itself (already the compute hub); a future self-hosted local web view of the desktop catalog would be the on-brand, custody-preserving su** · There is NO public iCloud Photos API — a structural wall. Our only analogue is the paired desktop node itself (already the compute hub); a future self-hosted local web view of the desktop catalog would be the on-brand, custody-preserving substitute. | blocked (cloud) |
| **iOS 26 Lock Screen Spatial Scene (depth-parallax wallpaper)** — iOS 26 turns a still into a depth-parallax Lock Screen wallpaper (reported iPhone 12+). It is wallpaper-only; there is NO public iOS API to generate the effect. | ⛔ can’t | P3 | We can't set wallpaper and have no depth-parallax viewing anywhere. — **Setting wallpaper is Apple-private; and Spatial3DImage/generate() ship only on visionOS, not iOS/macOS. To do parallax on iPhone we must build our OWN monocular depth estimation on the desktop pipeline and sync lightweight depth maps — an o** · Setting wallpaper is Apple-private; and Spatial3DImage/generate() ship only on visionOS, not iOS/macOS. To do parallax on iPhone we must build our OWN monocular depth estimation on the desktop pipeline and sync lightweight depth maps — an opening where Apple ships no API, viewable across iPhone/Mac/Windows. | blocked (platform API) |

## Where we already lead or match Apple

33 places we lead, 12 at parity — the honest other side of the ledger.

| Capability | Us | What Apple does | Why we lead / match |
|---|:--:|---|---|
| **Dark Interface** | ✅ have | Apple Photos fully supports Dark Mode; 'Dark Interface' is one of the nine declarable Accessibility Nutrition-Label features. | Confirm every screen (including desktop-preview and pairing surfaces) renders correctly in dark and meets 4.5:1, then declare Dark Interface. Cheap Nutrition-Label win. |
| **On-device flashing-lights dimming / Reduce Bright Effects (photosensitivity)** | ✅ have | iOS uses an on-device algorithm to detect flashing/strobing in supported media and auto-dims it in real time; Dim Flashing Lights and Reduce Bright Effects live under Display & Text Size. | Keep the living-gradient motion low-frequency and low-contrast-swing; honor Reduce Bright Effects where our chrome is bright. No video pipeline means the OS flashing detector has little of ours to act on. |
| **Auto-Play gating on Auto-Play Video Previews / Auto-Play Animated Images** | ✅ have | Accessibility → Motion gates autoplay via Auto-Play Video Previews (UIAccessibility.isVideoAutoplayEnabled) and Auto-Play Animated Images; Apple's HIG requires honoring these before autoplaying any audio/video. | If/when video or Live Photos are added, gate on BOTH Reduce Motion (RN gives this) AND Auto-Play Video Previews via a native module wrapping isVideoAutoplayEnabled + videoAutoplayStatusDidChangeNotification (no RN binding), and read ANIMATOR_DURATION_SCALE on Android. Not needed today. |
| **Color Filters compatibility** | ✅ have | Color Filters (color-blind/tint correction) apply globally; Apple warns 'Color filters can change the look of images and videos,' and unlike Smart Invert there is NO per-view exemption for photos. | No accessibilityIgnoresInvertColors analogue exists for Color Filters; it cannot be exempted by any app. Documentation-only awareness item. |
| **Hover Text** | ✅ have | Hover Text (zoom whatever is under the pointer into a separate high-res window) is a macOS-only feature; it does NOT exist on iOS. | Do NOT plan iOS Hover Text work. For the desktop companion separately: render real semantic DOM, expose app.setAccessibilitySupportEnabled, and wire prefers-reduced-motion/prefers-contrast. |
| **Live Recognition / Point & Speak gesture reservations** | ✅ have | With VoiceOver on, the system claims multi-finger gestures globally: two-finger scrub (escape), three-finger tap (repeat description), three-finger double-tap (Zoom toggle), four-finger triple-tap (Live Recognition). Live Recognition/Point & Speak is a camera/world feature, not a Photos one. | Constraint, not a build. Keep all custom gestures to one- or two-finger; never ship a three-finger gesture. Surface any complex gesture (multi-select drag, swipe-to-delete) additionally as accessibilityActions. |
| **Favorites (heart) as a boolean flag that write-throughs, plus Favorites collection + filter** | ✅ have | Favorites is a system collection, pinned by default, doubling as a filter; it's a boolean flag on the asset (PHAsset.isFavorite) — the ENTIRETY of the cross-app-writable tagging vocabulary on iOS (no colors/labels/stars pre-iOS 27). | Shipped and correct — PHAsset.isFavorite read / PHAssetChangeRequest.isFavorite write. This is the single organizational tag that round-trips into Apple Photos, and we already handle it. |
| **Base still formats (HEIC/HEIF, JPEG) — ingest & display** | ✅ have | Camera writes HEIC (High Efficiency) or JPEG (Most Compatible); Photos ingests, grids, and full-screen views every still, including the auxiliary-image-bearing containers. | Already shipped; HEIC decode handled via the expo-image-manipulator transcode per the known ph:// / HEIC gotcha. (Auxiliary images inside the HEIC are still discarded — those are the Portrait/HDR/spatial gaps above.) |
| **Macro & Night-mode stills** | ✅ have | Both produce ordinary HEIC/JPEG stills (Night can be ProRAW at 12 MP) with no special resource; Macro silently swaps to the Ultra Wide sensor and 48 MP silently falls back to 12 MP off the 1× Main camera. | No PHAssetMediaSubtype exists for either. Minor opportunity: reading pixelWidth/height to label the REAL resolution would be more honest than Apple's own UI about the silent 48→12 MP fallback. |
| **Screenshots — ingest + Screenshots album (incl. iOS 26 HDR HEIC screenshots)** | ✅ have | Screenshots ingest as .photoScreenshot and appear in a Screenshots media-types album; iOS 26's Screen Capture setting can make them HDR HEIC instead of PNG; the pane also holds Automatic Visual Look Up. | Already handled: screenshots ingest as images and the PhotoKit Screenshots smart album drill-in ships in Albums. HDR-HEIC screenshots render fine (just not with true HDR); Automatic Visual Look Up is a separate OCR/VLU domain. |
| **Persistent bottom tab bar as the primary navigation model** | ✅ have | iOS 26 restored a persistent floating tab bar (Library / Collections / Search) after iOS 18 deleted it to severe, sustained backlash; Federighi from the WWDC stage: 'Many of you missed using tabs.' The durable lesson is behavioral: ship a persistent tab bar and never take it away. | Already shipped as a 4-tab RN navigator (Library/Search/Albums/Desktop). Nothing to close. |
| **System Share Sheet (send out): AirDrop, AirPlay-to-TV, Save to Files, Copy, Messages** | ✅ have | Photos' Share button presents UIActivityViewController with single/multi/whole-collection modes, giving AirDrop (P2P encrypted), AirPlay to Apple TV / AirPlay-2 TVs, Save to Files, Copy, Add to Album, Messages, etc. for free. | Already shipped via RN's Share/UIActivityViewController. AirDrop and AirPlay come free with the sheet; no peer enumeration is possible for anyone. Add multi-select share and whole-album share for fuller parity. |
| **Sensitive-content detection on the user's OWN local camera roll** | ★ we lead | Apple deliberately draws its line at SHARED content: Sensitive Content Warning and Communication Safety classify Messages/AirDrop/Shared Albums/FaceTime only. Apple NEVER classifies the user's own local camera roll — not by SCW, not by Communication Safety, on any shipping OS. | SCA is categorically unusable as our engine (returns nothing on a default adult device, bare boolean with no score, forbids off-device transmission), so our classifier is the only architecture that works. Bring verdicts to mobile via the desktop oracle over LAN — but per Apple's purpose-limit, keep ALL cross-device safety state OURS (never an SCA-derived verdict on the wire). Positioning rules: never say 'scan' or 'on-device' (ML runs on the paired Mac). |
| **Gore / violence content classification (SCA detectedTypes .goreOrViolence)** | ★ we lead | iOS 27 (beta; GA ~Sept 2026) adds .goreOrViolence to SCA detectedTypes and Communication Safety will block gore/violence in SHARED images/videos. On the current shipping OS (iOS 26.5.2) there is NO first-party gore/violence signal on iPhone for anyone. | Steal Apple's interaction research regardless: acknowledge-then-reveal for sexually explicit; hard-block-by-default with a progressive slider reveal for gore/violence (Apple's rationale: immediate exposure to violent imagery can be traumatic). detectedTypes/SCVideoStreamAnalyzer themselves are iOS-27-beta / iOS-26-only and unusable to us anyway. |
| **SCA live-stream nudity analysis (SCVideoStreamAnalyzer) & the SCA framework generally** | ★ we lead | SensitiveContentAnalysis exposes on-device nudity detection to third parties (SCSensitivityAnalyzer.analyzeImage; SCVideoStreamAnalyzer for live streams, iOS 26.0-only). Gated behind SCW or Communication Safety; entitlement required. | Keep our own classifier (positively validated, not merely tolerated). If we ever adopt SCA for a secondary purpose, its verdicts must stay on the phone (never LAN/analytics/crash logs). SCVideoStreamAnalyzer is iOS-26-only and would only matter if we added a live-camera surface, which we don't have. |
| **Duplicates detection + Merge** | ★ we lead | Utilities > Duplicates finds duplicates and offers Merge (keeps highest quality). Opaque black box — no thresholds, no manual pairing, no choice of which copy survives, often empty/late on large libraries, and NO third-party API. | Shipped. Third-party cleaners own this category precisely because Apple exposes no API; our angle should be explicit thresholds + a 'keep this one' control. |
| **Paired-desktop collections uplink (browse the desktop catalog's collections)** | ★ we lead | No Apple equivalent — Apple has no concept of browsing a separate paired desktop's photo library/collections from the phone. | Shipped (companion HTTP: pair > catalog > previews > recognition oracle). This is a genuine structural advantage and the honest counterweight to the many misses above. |
| **Natural-language semantic search (text→image, core capability)** | ★ we lead | Free-text semantic search over the library ('Maya skateboarding in a tie-dye shirt'), gated to iPhone 15 Pro/16+, 16 languages, 7GB free, unavailable in mainland China; silently degrades to keyword search with no explanation on unsupported devices. Documented failures on time-of-day/lighting terms, color+object attribute binding ('yellow car' returns no cars), and in-image text. | Already shipped (react-native-executorch CLIP + op-sqlite/sqlite-vec). Honest caveat: CLIP ViT-B/32 shares Apple's attribute-binding weakness, so 'beat the retrieval quality' is aspirational without a stronger SigLIP-class model; the durable win is coverage + no-gate + transparency, not raw recall. |
| **Apple Intelligence device / language / region GATE (coverage)** | ★ we lead | All three Photos AI features hard-gated to iPhone 15 Pro / 16-or-later, device-language==Siri-language in one of 16 languages, 7GB free storage, and UNAVAILABLE in mainland China — with silent, unexplained degradation on excluded devices. | Inherent to our Vision/Core-ML/CLIP-on-device stack, which carries no Apple-Intelligence dependency. The atlas explicitly names this as Apple's self-inflicted, structural opening (§9.14). |
| **Privacy posture of on-device vs server photo analysis** | ★ we lead | Apple's Photos AI is a mix: Enhanced Visual Search sends encrypted embeddings to Apple servers (opt-OUT, undisclosed at setup — cost Apple trust in Jan 2025); iOS 27's best editing (High Quality Clean Up, Image Playground) runs on server diffusion metered behind iCloud+; ChatGPT features go to OpenAI. Apple cannot honestly say 'all local' for its headline features. | Already our architecture. Opportunity: make the on-device guarantee explicit in UI (the free 'explain why/where AI runs' win the atlas flags), and keep any future desktop-uplink or server feature opt-IN and disclosed. |
| **Image→image 'find similar / more like this'** | ★ we lead | Photos has no prominent first-party image→image 'find visually similar photos' KNN; its similarity smarts are indirect (Duplicates album, landmark match). | Already shipped (read the vec0 blob back and KNN over sqlite-vec). No dependency on Apple Intelligence. |
| **Desktop-oracle per-photo people + rich EXIF (faces, camera/lens/aperture/ISO/place)** | ★ we lead | Apple runs People & Pets recognition on-device for free on all AI devices; EXIF/camera metadata is shown natively. No paired-desktop concept. | This IS our architecture (SEC-09 companion HTTP uplink to the macOS suite). Lead is real but gated on the desktop being present/reachable; the decision-layer port is conformance-tested but not yet wired to live matches, and standalone people SEARCH (not just per-photo recognize) is still missing (see the person-name item). |
| **Indexing transparency & resumability** | ★ we lead | Opaque and fragile — long-standing reports of Photos 'endlessly analyzing the library' with no progress, no resumability, and no diagnosis, leaving search 'unusable' until it finishes. | Pure JS over our own op-sqlite ingest pipeline — add a visible progress/resumable-state indicator. No native work; converts a latent lead into a visible one. |
| **Byte-exact / no-transcode transfer + per-asset fidelity badge** | ★ we lead | Apple documents that AirDrop/Messages/email/USB 'might' transcode HEIF->JPEG / HEVC->H.264 with no UI indication; only iCloud Photos preserves original format/resolution. | Our LAN sync can be byte-exact (a fidelity claim Apple can't make about its own tooling) — but only if it copies every resource, not one rendered image (see PHAssetResource item). Surface a per-asset fidelity badge (Original/Transcoded/Flattened); cheap since we already hash for sync. Not yet fully surfaced in-app. |
| **Offline natural-language / semantic search** | ★ we lead | Apple's natural-language search is Apple-Intelligence-gated (A17 Pro + 8GB: iPhone 15 Pro/Pro Max and all 16/17), explicitly beta, 16 languages, region-limited; older devices get weak keyword search. Enhanced Visual Search is network-touching and region-gated. | Already shipped: on-device CLIP ViT-B/32, fully offline, no cloud, no Apple-Intelligence gate, every region, every device. Honest residual gaps vs Apple's search TAXONOMY remain (no place/date/business/OCR/media-type filters, no term-combining) but on availability and privacy we beat Apple outright. |
| **Sync observability + selective sync (LAN custody model)** | ★ we lead | iCloud Photos is all-or-nothing with automatic LRU eviction; you can't pin an album offline, can't see which items are stuck ('Syncing 2 items...' with no way to find them across 132k photos), and iOS 17.5 resurrected years-deleted photos — a deep trust wound. iOS 27 adds only a partial 'Sync Immediately' lever. | Our desktop-as-compute topology gives server-class sync without surrendering custody (the 'server' is the user's own Mac on their LAN). Opening not yet fully surfaced: a per-asset verified hash ledger, an explicit 'N items failed — here they are — retry' list, and selective pin-album-offline / sync-only-these-people. Ship the observability UI to make the lead legible. |
| **One-tap access to user Albums (Albums as a top-level destination)** | ★ we lead | Apple has NO Albums tab — Albums is a row you must scroll to inside Collections (3+ taps). Losing the dedicated Albums tab is the #1 named complaint of the iOS 18 redesign and iOS 26 did NOT fix it; the only workaround (pin it) is undiscoverable. | Already a dedicated bottom tab. Atlas §1.18 names this exact one-tap-Albums gap as a straight win over Apple. |
| **Paired-desktop uplink as a top-level navigation destination** | ★ we lead | No Apple equivalent — Photos has no concept of pairing to a trusted desktop that computes heavyweight ML the phone can't. | Already shipped (SEC-09 companion HTTP: pair → catalog → desktop previews → face-recognition oracle, cookie auth). A structural advantage with no Apple analogue. |
| **Persistent bottom tab bar / where Library sits (Library + Collections + Search)** | ★ we lead | iOS 26 restored a 2-tab Liquid-Glass tab bar (Library \| Collections) plus a separate bottom Search button after the iOS 18 no-tab-bar redesign became the app's loudest-ever backlash; the tab bar morphs into a nav bar exposing Months/Years on scroll. | Already implemented; the lesson from the atlas is behavioral (never remove the tab bar), which we satisfy. No work needed. |
| **Curated 'best shots' Years/Months view (suppresses screenshots, near-dupes, whiteboards, receipts)** | ★ we lead | Years and Months apply an opaque on-device aesthetic/curation score to show only 'best shots' and actively hide clutter. Apple exposes no API and no user control, and this silently-lossy default drives a large share of 'my photos are missing' complaints. | We already win by being complete-by-default. Adding an OPT-IN curated view (score + hide clutter) would need an on-device aesthetic model; CLIP embeddings we already compute + a screenshot/receipt classifier could approximate it. Worth an always-visible 'N hidden by curation — show' affordance if we ever add it. PhotoKit ships no such API (verified via atlas §2.19). |
| **Detail viewer core: full-screen, swipe paging, pinch/double-tap zoom, tap-to-toggle chrome** | ★ we lead | Tap a thumbnail → full-screen; swipe to page; pinch and double-tap to zoom within a photo; tap to hide/show controls; X to close. Well-liked — the baseline to MATCH. | Already implemented (native pinch-zoom, swipe paging, tap-to-toggle chrome, zoom-open-from-cell). The one missing sub-piece is the filmstrip scrubber (separate row). |
| **Duplicates detection + merge** | ★ we lead | Collections → Utilities → Duplicates finds duplicate sets and Merge combines best pixels + union of relevant data (reversible via Recently Deleted). Famously slow to populate (days-to-weeks on large libraries), catches only near-exact dupes, undocumented criteria, and users can't see why two matched or force a re-scan. | Already implemented (CLIP embedding near-dup + merge). Opportunities to extend our lead: show WHY two matched, let users choose which copy survives, and a force-rescan — all things Apple can't do. PhotoKit exposes no Duplicates API, so this is entirely our own moat. |
| **Liquid Glass grid chrome (translucent contextual tab/nav bar)** | ★ we lead | Photos is a flagship Liquid Glass app: translucent tab bar/search/select buttons that show photos behind them and resize as you scroll. Known complaints: legibility over busy photos and controls that feel unstable as they resize. | Intentional divergence; already shipped. We use pure RN Animated (no blur module), so we're not mimicking translucency — which the atlas endorses. No work needed. |
| **No-gate, no-cloud on-device curation substrate (CLIP + desktop oracle running on ANY iPhone)** | ★ we lead | Apple's flagship curation intelligence (Create a memory movie, Natural Language Search, and iOS 27 'smarter memories') is HARD-GATED to iPhone 15 Pro / 16 or later with Apple Intelligence enabled + ~7GB free + 16 supported languages, and parts may use Private Cloud Compute. The auto-memory/movie base works on all iOS 26 phones but the smart layer does not. | Already shipped: offline executorch CLIP ViT-B/32 + encrypted on-device SQLite replica, no server dependency. This is the platform advantage to build the whole curation domain on. Level-field bonus: Apple exposes zero curation via PhotoKit (no PHMemory/featured/aesthetics-score symbols), so we're not 'behind on data' — nobody can read Apple's graph. |
| **Licensed desktop face/pet oracle as the people-anchor for curation** | ★ we lead | Apple's Memories/Featured/People-search are all built on its on-device face & pet recognition graph, which runs entirely locally but is NOT exposed to third parties (no API to read identities). | Differentiator with no Apple equivalent, but only available when a desktop is paired; a stills/date/place fallback is needed for phone-only users. The recognition itself is the desktop's licensed oracle, not on-device. |
| **Confidence surfacing / tunable, explainable matching** | ★ we lead | NONE. Across ~12 curation levers, not one surfaces why two clusters joined, how confident a match is, or lets the user steer a threshold. Apple structurally chose not to build a visible decision layer. | The port consumes desktop-oracle scores; realizing it means wiring live matches through Platt/AS-Norm and rendering confidence. Conceptually beats Apple; the only work is connecting it to live recognition. |
| **Un-merge / reversible merges with provenance** | ★ we lead | NONE on iOS. There is no un-merge anywhere; a bad merge permanently poisons the cluster centroid and future matching compounds the error. The only 'fix' is marking every photo 'This is Not [name]' one by one. | Requires the writable graph + oracle; non-destructive merge/provenance is the design intent of the decision-layer port. Not shipped. |
| **Cross-age matching / age-conditioned linking** | ★ we lead | Architecturally absent — Apple's own paper shows conservative permanent first-pass merges, running-average centroids, within-moment-only clothing cues, and face-only HAC with no age-invariant loss, no age-conditioned matching, no cross-age linking pass. Apple never claims cross-age robustness. | Cross-age banding already ported; realize by wiring live desktop matches. This is a structural moat: Apple's published pipeline has no path to it. (Note honestly: measure sibling/lookalike conflation too — Apple Community reports it's at least as common a failure.) |
| **People-graph portability / export** | ★ we lead | Locked in — no people export on iOS at all; years of naming/merging are trapped. The only escape is an unsupported macOS SQLite read (ZPERSON/ZDETECTEDFACE). | The osxphotos import path is macOS-only and unsupported/schema-fragile, but it runs once on the paired Mac; impossible on iOS (DB sandboxed). Turns Apple's biggest lock-in into our funnel. |
| **Natural-language semantic search (core text->image)** | ★ we lead | Apple Intelligence 'Natural language search in Photos' — describe a photo in words, matched against on-device image-language embeddings AND joined with the named-people index (e.g. 'Maya skateboarding in a tie-dye shirt'). Gated to iPhone 15 Pro / 16 / 17 only, 16 languages, unavailable in China mainland, still labelled 'beta'. | Already shipped (react-native-executorch CLIP + sqlite-vec). Person-conditioning needs a person index (needs-desktop oracle) fused with CLIP scores. |
| **Category / scene search (beach, sunset, food, dog...)** | ★ we lead | Type a 'Category' term; matched against a CLOSED, undocumented, fixed scene taxonomy. Queries outside it ('Rome monuments') silently return nothing; users can't see or extend the vocabulary. | Already shipped via CLIP. Could add curated category chips (client-side canned queries) cheaply. |
| **Find Similar (image->image KNN over the whole library)** | ★ we lead | Apple Photos has NO first-class 'find visually similar to THIS photo' over your library — only a Duplicates detector and visual-intelligence's 'visually similar images' from a screenshot (web-facing). You cannot pick a library photo and get its nearest neighbours. | Already shipped. Clear, defensible lead over Apple. |
| **Desktop uplink: full-library desktop search + face-recognition oracle + EXIF/collections** | ★ we lead | No Apple equivalent. Apple's SDK gives third parties zero face identity, and Apple's own people/scene/OCR index is neither exportable nor queryable — locked to the device. | By design (licensed heavyweight ML on the desktop oracle). The remaining work is surfacing desktop-computed person/place/OCR indices AS native phone search tokens, not just a separate desktop-search screen. |
| **At-rest privacy / encryption posture (vs iCloud Standard Data Protection & ADP)** | ★ we lead | iCloud Photos is NOT end-to-end encrypted by default — Apple holds the keys (in transit & on server). ADP is opt-in, has total-recovery burden, disables iCloud.com by default, excludes Managed/child accounts, is unavailable to new UK users since Feb 2025, and even then leaves photo byte-checksums, favorite/hidden/deleted flags, and dates/view-counts under Apple-held keys. Shared Albums are never E2E. | Already shipped (encrypted on-device replica, offline-first). No Apple cloud dependency means ADP's holes simply don't apply to us. |
| **Offline availability & 'make available offline' pinning** | ★ we lead | Apple has NO offline-pinning control anywhere in Photos: with Optimize Storage on by default and opaque LRU eviction, users can board a plane and find their library is proxies, and there is no 'keep this album offline' toggle and no local-vs-cloud indicator. | Already shipped and a genuine lead. Strengthen it by pairing with the Optimize-Storage fix so 'available offline' also means 'full-res original pulled and pinned' (creationRequestForDownloadJob can pre-materialize, though the system may purge it). |

## Completeness critic — features the domain pass under-covered

An adversarial pass caught these additional Apple capabilities worth tracking:

| Feature — what Apple does | Why it was missed | Sev | Build |
|---|---|:--:|---|
| **Assign to Contact / Use as Contact Photo** — The Photos share/activity row includes "Assign to Contact": pick a contact, crop and scale the image in a built-in framing UI, and set it as that contact's photo (atlas §11.1, line 4371). | The gap list enumerates the share/activity surface only as "System Share Sheet (AirDrop, AirPlay-to-TV, Save to Files, Copy, Messages) = have" and never lists the two other first-class activity-row items, Assign to Contact and Print. The app's actual share path is RN Share.share({url}), a reduced URL sheet that does not reliably surface Assign to Contact, so this is a genuine omission, not a covered "have." | P3 | native · moderate |
| **Print a photo (AirPrint) from the share/activity row** — "Print" is a standard Photos activity → AirPrint with copies, paper size, scale and layout options; Apple ships a dedicated "Print photos" AirPrint guide (atlas §11.12 line 4565, §11.1 line 4371). | No entry anywhere in the gap list mentions printing; the share-sheet "have" item omits Print, and RN's URL Share does not guarantee the Print activity. Zero printing coverage across all 19 domains. | P3 | native · moderate |
| **Print projects (photo books, cards, calendars)** — Apple Photos' project/print-product creation (books, cards, calendars, wall art) — the atlas notes macOS Photos "has more export and print capability" (§14.9 line 5936); on iPhone this exists only via third-party Print Product share-sheet extensions. | The task explicitly flagged "print projects" as an obscure-but-real feature to catch, and the gap list has no project/print-product entry at all. Genuinely absent, though it is largely a macOS surface with no native iOS equivalent. | P3 | — |
| **Live Text OCR and subject-lift on a PAUSED VIDEO frame** — Live Text (video OCR since iOS 16) and "Lift a subject from the photo OR video background" both operate on any paused video frame — Apple: "Open a photo in full screen, or pause a video on any frame" (atlas §3.7 line 950, §11.11 line 4544). The support page is literally titled with "or video background." | The gap list scopes Live Text/OCR and subject-lift to still photos only (both listed as "missing" for stills); the video-frame surface is never captured — and it is doubly gated because video ingestion/playback is itself marked "missing," so no path to it exists. | P2 | native · hard |
| **Live Stickers (subject cutout with effects + system sticker keyboard)** — "Add Sticker" (iOS 17+) turns a lifted subject into a reusable sticker — with effects, animated for Live Photos — saved to the system sticker drawer/emoji keyboard and usable across Messages, Mail and any text field (atlas §11.11 line 4551, §11.4 line 4426). | The list captures only a raw "subject cut-out / stickers." The distinct surfaces — sticker effects, the Live-Photo-animated sticker, and registration into the system-wide sticker/emoji keyboard drawer — are not represented; the keyboard-drawer half has no third-party registration path and is easy to mis-assume as done. | P3 | native · hard |
| **Curated "best shots" Years/Months view — mis-classified as we-lead** — Apple's Years and Months library levels show curated best-shot picks per period, actively suppressing screenshots, near-duplicates, whiteboards and receipts via an on-device aesthetics score (atlas §2.2 lines 493-509). | The gap list marks "Curated best-shots Years/Months view" as "we-lead" while separately marking the prerequisite "Library zoom hierarchy: Years/Months/All" as "missing." These contradict — you cannot lead on a curated Years/Months surface you do not render. It should be missing/partial, not we-lead. | P2 | native · moderate |

## Feasibility rollup — what closing the gap actually takes

- **native · easy** — 84 missing/partial items (25 at P0/P1). e.g. Accessibility Nutrition Labels — the actual enforcement bar (9 declarable features); Crop — manual drag/pinch with default aspect lock; Smart Invert image exemption (accessibilityIgnoresInvertColors); Reduced Motion handling for meaningful vs decorative motion; iCloud-optimized / original-not-local ingest handling; A settings / account surface (any at all)
- **native · moderate** — 87 missing/partial items (24 at P0/P1). e.g. Screen Time Photos lock — PHAuthorizationStatus.restricted terminal state; Video (HEVC/H.264 .MOV) — ingest, grid presence, and playback; Video, Live Photo & burst ingestion + playback in the library; Larger Text / Dynamic Type to 200%+ with layout reflow; 'Not in an Album' filter / an unfiled-photo inbox; App Intents 'Photos' domain adoption
- **native · hard** — 76 missing/partial items (23 at P0/P1). e.g. Adjust — the 16-item slider strip (Auto/Enhance + 15 tonal/color sliders); Edit mode: entry point + staged-edit session chrome; Live Text / OCR text-in-photo search (dimension 7); Memory movie playback (auto-edited, beat-synced, music-scored montage with title card); Revert to Original + non-destructive edit stack (original + recipe + current render); Accessible virtualized photo grid — VoiceOver/TalkBack collection semantics
- **needs desktop** — 48 missing/partial items (13 at P0/P1). e.g. Person / people (named face) search; People & Pets collection + Groups (browsable on-device people/pet graph); Conversational 'Ask Photos' / Siri AI personal-context photo search; Don't Feature a Person / Never Feature This Person (emotional safety); Face recognition / clustering into identities (the people graph); People & Pets collection (browsable identity grid)
- **blocked (cloud)** — 15 missing/partial items (2 at P0/P1). e.g. iCloud sync of albums across devices; iCloud Photos bidirectional sync (one library across all devices)
- **blocked (platform API)** — 38 missing/partial items (3 at P0/P1). e.g. Recently Deleted (30-day trash: view, recover, permanently delete); Shared Albums (legacy iCloud Photo Sharing): create, invite participants, post, permissions, comments/likes, activity feed; iCloud Shared Photo Library (a separate, full-fidelity second library with MOVE semantics)
- **—** — 2 missing/partial items (0 at P0/P1). e.g.
